The administrative penalty shall not exceed 100 % of the amounts based on the area declared.

(2.)[^c000004] Where no administrative penalty has yet been imposed on the beneficiary under paragraph 1 for over-declaration of areas for the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e29e21591238455432a96ce7726e4dd0] aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2ffbb624d99986f5b27a1c68534db78d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e29e21591238455432a96ce7726e4dd0] support measure concerned, the administrative penalty referred to in that paragraph shall be reduced by 50 % if the difference between the area declared and the area determined does not exceed 10 % of the area determined. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e29e21591238455432a96ce7726e4dd0]The administrative penalty referred to in paragraph 1 is to be reduced by 50% if the difference between the area declared and the area determined does not exceed 10% of the area determined.

(3.)[^key-8ab1c64fe773780940058699e306e7dc] Where a beneficiary had his administrative penalty reduced in accordance with paragraph 2 and another administrative penalty as referred to in this Article and in Article 21 is to be imposed on that beneficiary for the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8ab1c64fe773780940058699e306e7dc] aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2ffbb624d99986f5b27a1c68534db78d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8ab1c64fe773780940058699e306e7dc] support measure concerned in respect of the following claim year, he shall pay the full administrative penalty in respect of that following claim year and shall pay the amount by which the administrative penalty calculated in accordance with paragraph 1 had been reduced in accordance with paragraph 2. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8ab1c64fe773780940058699e306e7dc]

(4.)[^key-6803a5dafd6eb229107a702cf70863f0] If the amount calculated in accordance with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6803a5dafd6eb229107a702cf70863f0] paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5c1e71db7221456dc7206b67055319d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6803a5dafd6eb229107a702cf70863f0]paragraphs 1 and 2 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6803a5dafd6eb229107a702cf70863f0] cannot be fully off-set in the course of the three calendar years following the calendar year of the finding, in accordance with Article 28 of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 908/2014, the outstanding balance shall be cancelled. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-6803a5dafd6eb229107a702cf70863f0]

##### Article19a. Administrative penalties in cases of over-declaration of areas for the farm sustainability transition payment scheme, the re-distributive payment, the young farmers scheme and voluntary coupled support[^M_E_1ee60cf7-8101-403b-c330-6376d75b41b2][^key-9ba07445290971e0244e9ecab7edeb4a][^key-b58e2ef3c845d607b406b39165990c9b]

*Extent: N.I.*

(1) If, in respect of a crop group as referred to in Article 17(1), the area declared for the aid schemes provided for in Chapters 1, 2 and 5 of Title III and in Title IV of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 exceeds the area determined in accordance with Article 18 of this Regulation, the aid or support shall be calculated on the basis of the area determined reduced by 1,5 times the difference found if that difference is more than either 3 % of the area determined or 2 hectares.

The administrative penalty shall not exceed 100 % of the amounts based on the area determined.

(2) The administrative penalty referred to in paragraph 1 will be reduced by 50% if the difference between the area declared and the area determined does not exceed 10% of the area determined.

##### Article 20. Administrative penalties concerning the crop specific payment for cotton[^key-6475f19d7dd2749c2d30fff8e9fc8c6f]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

##### Article 21 (affected by repeal, see note). Administrative penalties, other than over-declarations of areas, concerning the payments for young farmers under Chapter V of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013[^M_F_640e1e9c-cc37-4e82-f8d4-718323a7802d]

(1.) Without prejudice to the administrative penalties applicable in accordance with Article 19, where it is established that the beneficiary does not comply with the obligations referred to in Article 50(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and Article 49 of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014, the aid for young farmers shall not be paid or shall be withdrawn in full. Moreover, where it is established that the beneficiary provided false evidence for the purpose of proving compliance with the obligations, a penalty corresponding to 20 % of the amount the beneficiary has, or would otherwise have received as a payment for young farmers pursuant to Article 50(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be applied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_640e1e9c-cc37-4e82-f8d4-718323a7802d]

(2.) If the amount of the undue payments and the administrative penalties referred to in paragraph 1 cannot be fully off-set in the course of the three calendar years following the calendar year of the finding, in accordance with Article 28 of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 908/2014, the outstanding balance shall be cancelled. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_640e1e9c-cc37-4e82-f8d4-718323a7802d]

#### SECTION 3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Payment for agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

##### Article 22 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). General principles (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537][^c000005][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406][^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

For the purposes of this Section, where the same area is determined for more than one of the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment as referred to in Article 43(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, that area shall be taken into account separately for each of those practices in view of calculating the payment for agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment, hereinafter referred to as ‘the greening payment’. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

Reduction of the greening payment in case of non-compliance with the ecological focus area requirements (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] The ecological focus area required in accordance with Article 46(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, hereinafter referred to as ‘the ecological focus area required’, shall be calculated on the basis of the total area of arable land determined and including, if applicable pursuant to Article 46(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the areas determined as referred to in points (c), (d), (g) and (h) of the first subparagraph of Article 46(2) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] If the ecological focus area required exceeds the ecological focus area determined taking account of the weighting of ecological focus areas provided for in Article 46(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment in accordance with Article 23 of this Regulation shall be reduced by 10 times the ecological focus area not found. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

For the purposes of the first subparagraph, the ecological focus area determined shall not exceed the share of the ecological focus areas declared in the total area of arable land declared. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] Where a beneficiary has been found non-compliant with the ecological focus area requirements as described in this Article for three years, the area by which the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment is to be reduced in accordance with paragraph 2 for the subsequent years shall be multiplied by 2. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

##### Article 27 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Maximum reduction of the greening payment (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] The sum of the reductions calculated in accordance with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] Article *(Extent: S)*[^key-000a579671bf57e273b94cde421bd6b2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 26 expressed in hectares shall not exceed the total number of hectares of arable land determined including, if applicable pursuant to Article 46(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the areas determined as referred to in points (c), (d), (g) and (h) of the first subparagraph of Article 46(2) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] Without prejudice to the application of administrative penalties applicable in accordance with Article 28, the total reduction calculated in accordance with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] Articles 25 and *(Extent: S)*[^key-cecabf04364a1f379b321bba52bdee5e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 26 shall not exceed the greening payment calculated in accordance with Article 23. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

##### Article 28 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Administrative penalties as regards the greening payment (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] If the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment in accordance with Article 23 differs from the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment after application of Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 25 *(Extent: S)*[^key-830f6df8a11397d63c03eee61b95ca13] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] to 27, the greening payment shall be calculated on this later area reduced by twice the difference established if that difference is more than either 3 % or two hectares, but no more than 20 % of the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment after application of Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 25 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] to 27. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

If the difference is more than 20 %, no aid shall be granted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

If the difference is more than 50 %, no aid shall be granted. Moreover, the beneficiary shall be subject to an additional penalty equal to the amount of aid corresponding to the difference between the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment in accordance with Article 23 and the area to be used for calculation of the greening payment after application of Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 25 *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] to 27. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] If the beneficiary does not declare all his area under arable land with the result that he would have been exempted from the obligations provided for in Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] [^key-b89fcb90367cf0b2169b30ceaa4ebeeb]... 45 and 46 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, and/or he does not declare all his permanent grassland which is environmentally sensitive in accordance with Article 45(1) of that Regulation and the non-declared area is more than 0,1 ha, the area to be used for the calculation of the greening payment after application of Articles *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] 25 *(Extent: S)*[^key-9c3934405883344780c765b0815c473e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] to 27 of this Regulation shall be further reduced by 10 %. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-011668369cadf79370eff56e8d3209fb][^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] In accordance with Article 77(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the administrative penalty calculated in accordance with paragraphs 1 and 2 shall be divided by 4 and limited to 25% of the amount of the greening payment to which the farmer concerned would have been entitled in accordance with Article 23. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(4. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406] If the amount of the administrative penalties calculated in accordance with paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 cannot be fully off-set in the course of the three calendar years following the calendar year of the finding, in accordance with Article 28 of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 908/2014, the outstanding balance shall be cancelled. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aef92bb776f76f537ea7d829c5310537] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-83a38ce623135c52af5c1daab4c77406]

(3a. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] Where animals have been moved to other locations than those notified in accordance with point (d) of Article 21(1) of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 809/2014 during the period determined by the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] relevant authority[^key-11c7cb151b38df5468ba7fe3a9c139ed] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] referred to in that point, they shall be regarded as determined if an immediate localisation of those animals within the holding was made during the on-the-spot check. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

(4. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] Where cases of non-compliances with regard to the system for the identification and registration for bovine animals are found, the following shall apply: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

- ((a)) a bovine animal present on the holding which has lost one of the two ear tags shall be considered as determined provided that it is clearly and individually identified by the other elements of the system for the identification and registration of bovine animals referred to in points (b), (c) and (d) of the first paragraph of Article 3 of Regulation (EC) No 1760/2000; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]
- ((b)) where one single bovine animal present on the holding has lost two ear tags it shall be considered as determined provided that the animal can still be identified by the register, animal passport, database or other means laid down in Regulation (EC) No 1760/2000 and provided that the animal keeper can provide evidence that he has already taken action to remedy the situation before the announcement of the on-the-spot check; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]
- ((c)) where the non-compliances found relate to incorrect entries in the register, the animal passports or the computerised database for animals, but are not of relevance for the verification of the respect of the eligibility conditions *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] other than that referred to in Article 53(4) of Delegated Regulation (EU) No 639/2014 under the aid scheme or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-0b05b47e898bbe62aa1666c4e12d8f93] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] support measure concerned, the animal concerned shall only be considered as not determined if such incorrect entries are found during at least two checks within a period of 24 months. In all other cases the animals concerned shall be considered as not determined after the first finding. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

The entries in, and notifications to, the system for the identification and registration of bovine animals may be adjusted at any time in cases of obvious errors recognised by the competent authority. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

(5. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1] An ovine or caprine animal present on the holding which has lost one ear tag shall be considered as determined provided that the animal can still be identified by a first means of identification in accordance with Article 4(2)(a) of Regulation (EC) No 21/2004 and provided that all other requirements of the system for the identification and registration of ovine and caprine animals are fulfilled. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

##### Article 32 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Exceptions from the application of administrative penalties in cases of natural circumstances[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

The administrative penalties provided for in Article 31 shall not apply in cases where the beneficiary is unable to comply with the eligibility criteria, commitments or other obligations as a result of natural circumstances affecting the herd or flock, provided that he has informed the competent authority in writing within ten working days of finding any reduction in the number of animals. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

Without prejudice to the actual circumstances to be taken into account in individual cases, the competent authorities may recognise natural circumstances affecting the herd or flock consisting of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

- ((a)) the death of an animal as a consequence of a disease; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]
- ((b)) the death of an animal following an accident for which the beneficiary cannot be held responsible. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5901770bfc3bde05e6239cdb934f8bf1]

## TITLE III — SPECIFIC PROVISIONS FOR RURAL DEVELOPMENT SUPPORT MEASURES

### Article 35. Non-compliance with the eligibility criteria other than the size of area or number of animals, commitments or other obligations

(1.) The support claimed shall *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-aabef951fd687c90efc582096097dcdc]may *(Extent: W)* be refused or withdrawn in full where the eligibility criteria are not complied with.

(2.) The support claimed shall *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-b8aaafc93f9ce56044690f532793a296]may *(Extent: W)* be refused or be withdrawn in full or in part where the following commitments or other obligations are not complied with:

- ((a)) commitments established in the rural development programme; or
- ((b)) where relevant, other obligations of the operation established by the law applying in the constituent nation[^key-03cc01c8b42a87fe1326af7cc0cd1ee3] or established in the rural development programme, in particular public procurement, State aid *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-4fc579503e86e0f94831228de45c296c] and other obligatory standards and requirements.

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede] When deciding on the rate of refusal or withdrawal of support following the non-compliance with the commitments or other obligations referred to in paragraph 2, the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede] relevant authority[^key-90e35d2b4323a405127e8d8dbbe4d283] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]shall *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-bf04ec185cc977be2fc5a4943b1e31db] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]may *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede] take account of the severity, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede] extent, duration and reoccurrence *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-ad0cf5b01375b1df5ae7bf227cf9ae5d] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]extent and duration *(Extent: W)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede] of the non-compliance related to conditions for support referred to in paragraph 2. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]

The severity of the non-compliance shall depend, in particular, on the importance of the consequences of the non-compliance, taking into account the objectives of the commitments or obligations that were not met. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]

The extent of the non-compliance shall depend, in particular, on its effect on the operation as a whole. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]

The duration shall depend, in particular, on the length of time for which the effect lasts or the possibility of terminating this effect by reasonable means. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]

The reoccurrence shall depend on whether similar non-compliances have been found earlier during the last four years or during the whole programming period 2014-2020 in case of the same beneficiary and the same measure or type of operation or in the case of the programming period 2007-2013, the similar measure *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-260245a68e595ccc662d73182374fbb0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-93bdbe000603beba947898e53a918ede]

(3. (Extent: E)) When deciding on the rate of refusal or withdrawal of support following the non-compliance with the commitments or other obligations referred to in paragraph 2, the relevant authority may take account of:— *(Extent: E)*

- the circumstances that led to the non-compliance and whether it was caused by the intentional actions of the beneficiary or due to that person’s recklessness or negligence; *(Extent: E)*
- any steps taken by the beneficiary to report a change of circumstance or notify the Authority of the non-compliance within a reasonable period; *(Extent: E)*
- any failure on the part of the beneficiary to co-operate with an on-the-spot check; *(Extent: E)*
- any past conduct of the beneficiary during the period of the commitment and whether a similar non-compliance has previously occurred; *(Extent: E)*
- the nature of the non-compliance and whether it has caused widespread or irreparable damage; *(Extent: E)*
- the extent to which it is desirable, practicable or economic to permit the beneficiary to rectify the non-compliance; *(Extent: E)*
- the consequences of the non-compliance and whether it renders the purpose of the commitment unachievable within the timescales or finances of the commitment or to the standards agreed when the commitment was undertaken. *(Extent: E)*

(4.) In case of multiannual commitments or payments, withdrawals based on the criteria referred to in paragraph 3 shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-25ea96ea4891935823f5e7c8aa32ed4e]may *(Extent: E)* also apply to the amounts already paid in the previous years for the same operation.

(5.) In case the overall assessment based on the criteria referred to in paragraph 3 leads to establishing a serious non-compliance, the support shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-30ddfab612c6897a4a241ae851b5b21f]may *(Extent: E)* be refused or withdrawn in full. Furthermore, the beneficiary *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5] shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-30ddfab612c6897a4a241ae851b5b21f] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5]may *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5] be excluded from *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5] the same measure or type of operation *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-88d07ced3afa9447231bd290b15062ce] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5]any financial assistance scheme(s) under section 1 of the Agriculture Act 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5] for the calendar year of the finding and for the following calendar year. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8aef04b9b975145bbb8d29115f5935f5]

(6.) Where it is established that the beneficiary provided false evidence for the purpose of receiving the support or failed to provide the necessary information due to negligence, the support shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-70a305f37fc9ab6e7a9d191191016f0e]may *(Extent: E)* be refused or withdrawn in full. Furthermore, the beneficiary shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-70a305f37fc9ab6e7a9d191191016f0e]may *(Extent: E)* be excluded from the same measure or type of operation *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c1a54c26ff2322f8142efc578ab76768]any financial assistance scheme(s) under section 1 of the Agriculture Act 2020 *(Extent: E)* for the calendar year of finding and for the following calendar year.

(7. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-2e08fd03ad176f118b9862ad96e99cc2] If the withdrawals and administrative penalties referred to in paragraphs 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 cannot be fully off-set in the course of the three calendar years following the calendar year of the finding, in accordance with Article 28 of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 908/2014, the outstanding balance shall be cancelled. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-2e08fd03ad176f118b9862ad96e99cc2]

### Article 36. Suspending the support

The paying agency may suspend the support relating to certain expenditure where a non-compliance resulting in an administrative penalty is detected. The suspension shall be lifted by the paying agency as soon as the beneficiary proves to the satisfaction of the competent authority that the situation has been remedied. The maximum period of suspension shall not exceed three months. The relevant authority[^key-1d8bb630508ce689b05ca1be6ab1251e] may also set shorter maximum periods depending on the type of operations and the effects of the non-compliance in question.

The paying agency may only suspend support where the non-compliance does not prejudice the achievement of the overall purpose of the operation concerned, and if it is expected that the beneficiary is able to remedy the situation during the maximum period defined.

### CHAPTER II (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — CALCULATION AND APPLICATION OF ADMINISTRATIVE PENALTIES (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e][^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

#### Article 38 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). General rules concerning non-compliance (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e][^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] The ‘reoccurrence’ of a non-compliance means the non-compliance with the same requirement or standard determined more than once within a consecutive period of three calendar years, provided that the beneficiary has been informed of a previous non-compliance and, as the case may be, has had the possibility to take the necessary measures to terminate that previous non-compliance. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] For the purpose of determining the reoccurrence of a non-compliance, non-compliances determined in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 1122/2009 shall be taken into account and, in particular, GAEC 3, as listed in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, shall be considered equivalent to SMR 2 of Annex II to Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 in its version in force on 21 December 2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-7c15fc5b799890543f204c66209dab8b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] The ‘extent’ of a non-compliance shall be determined taking account, in particular, of whether the non-compliance has a far-reaching impact or whether it is limited to the farm itself. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] The ‘severity’ of a non-compliance shall depend, in particular, on the importance of the consequences of the non-compliance taking account of the aims of the requirement or standard concerned. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(4. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] Whether a non-compliance is of ‘permanence’ shall depend, in particular, on the length of time for which the effect lasts or the potential for terminating those effects by reasonable means. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(5. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] For the purposes of this Chapter, non-compliances shall be deemed to be ‘determined’ if they are established as a consequence of any kind of controls carried out in accordance with this Regulation or after having been brought to the attention of the competent control authority or, where applicable, the paying agency, in whatever other way. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

#### Article 39 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Calculation and application of administrative penalties in the case of negligence (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e][^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] Where a non-compliance determined results from the negligence of the beneficiary, a reduction shall be applied. That reduction shall *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] , as a general rule, be 3 % *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-94ce08b119a9485adfe8905730c455d4] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] be a percentage *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] of the total amount resulting from the payments and annual premiums indicated in Article 92 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

However, the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-84947d3bcdc68a3e7f9886380e64f3cd] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] The *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] paying agency may, on the basis of the assessment of the importance of the non-compliance provided by the competent control authority in the evaluation part of the control report taking into account the criteria referred to in Article 38(1) to (4), decide *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] either to reduce that percentage to 1 % or to increase it to 5 % *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-148dc418f0960f02e740ceb001c2f12b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] that the percentage reduction will be 1%, 2%, 3%, 4% or 5% *(Extent: E)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] of the total amount referred to in the first subparagraph or, in the cases where provisions relating to the requirement or standard in question leave a margin not to further pursue the non-compliance found or in the cases for which support is granted according to Article 17(5) and (6) of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, not to impose any reductions at all. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] Where a *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] relevant authority[^key-dd9a56f2cb6fa68f8377d148118eaab0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] decides not to apply an administrative penalty pursuant to Article 97(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and the beneficiary has not remedied the situation within a deadline set by the competent authority, the administrative penalty shall be applied. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

The deadline set by the competent authority shall not be later than the end of the year following the one in which the finding was made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] Where a *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] relevant authority[^key-f936a4535f96efa8d78a67cfc863bdbd] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] makes use of the option provided for in the second subparagraph of Article 99(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and the beneficiary has not remedied the situation within a deadline set by the competent authority, a reduction of at least 1 % as provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be applied retroactively in relation to the year of the initial finding when the early warning system was applied, if the non-compliance is found not to have been remedied during a maximum period of three consecutive calendar years calculated from and including that year. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

The deadline set by the competent authority shall not be later than the end of the year following the one in which the finding was made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

A non-compliance which has been remedied by the beneficiary within the deadline set shall not be considered as a non-compliance for the purpose of establishing reoccurrence in accordance with paragraph 4. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

(4. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] Without prejudice to cases of intentional non-compliance, the reduction to be applied in respect of the first reoccurrence of the same non-compliance in accordance with paragraph 1 shall be multiplied by the factor three. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

In case of further reoccurrences, the multiplication factor three shall be applied each time to the result of the reduction fixed in respect of the previous reoccurrence. The maximum reduction shall, however, not exceed 15 % of the total amount referred to in paragraph 1. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

Once the maximum percentage of 15 % has been reached, the paying agency shall inform the beneficiary concerned that if the same non-compliance is determined again, the beneficiary shall be considered to have acted intentionally within the meaning of Article 40. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

Once the maximum percentage of 15% has been reached, the paying agency shall inform the beneficiary concerned that if the same non-compliance is determined again, the beneficiary shall be considered to have acted intentionally within the meaning of Article 40. *(Extent: N.I.)*[^key-ff533595fc6ef5ba07b917fe8d2c5ac1]

#### Article 40 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Calculation and application of administrative penalties in cases of intentional non-compliance (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e][^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

Where the non-compliance determined has been committed intentionally by the beneficiary, the reduction to be applied to the total amount referred to in Article 39(1) shall, as a general rule, be 20 % of that total amount. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

However, the paying agency may, on the basis of the assessment of the importance of the non-compliance provided by the competent control authority in the evaluation part of the control report taking into account the criteria referred to in Article 38(1) to (4), decide to reduce that percentage to no less than 15 % or to increase that percentage to up to 100 % of that total amount. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

#### Article 41 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Cumulation of administrative penalties (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e][^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

Where a case of non-compliance within the meaning of point (2)(b) of the second subparagraph of Article 2(1) also constitutes a non-compliance within the meaning of point (2)(a) of the second subparagraph of Article 2(1), the administrative penalties shall be applied in accordance with the rules laid down *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e] [^key-4d22e2760e100d216609a019f7192039]... on the basis of Article 77(8)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8b1895e2ef68f178a0e1909158e91d4e]

## TITLE V (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note) — TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

### Article 42 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Transitional rules as regards cross-compliance (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-cca2eb41e31c26cb781a9b2482d53dd6][^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

(1. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed] As regards cross-compliance obligations of beneficiaries of measures implemented pursuant to Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005, the rules on the control system and administrative penalties laid down in this Regulation and in the implementing acts adopted by the Commission on the basis of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 shall apply. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

(2. (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed] For non-compliances with cross-compliance obligations for which administrative penalties were not applied since they were falling under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed] *de minimis *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]* rule referred to in Article 23(2) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 or in Article 51(2) of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 respectively, the second subparagraph of Article 97(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 shall apply as regards the obligation on the control authority to take actions necessary to verify that the beneficiary has remedied the findings of non-compliance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

### Article 43 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Repeal (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

Regulations (EC) No 1122/2009 and (EU) No 65/2011 are repealed with effect from 1 January 2015. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

However, those Regulations shall continue to apply to: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

- ((a)) aid applications for direct payments lodged in respect of premium periods starting before 1 January 2015; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]
- ((b)) payment claims made and application for support relating to the year 2014 and earlier years and payment claims relating to the year 2015 under Article 66(2) of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]
- ((c)) the control system and administrative penalties as regards cross-compliance obligations of farmers under Articles 85t and 103z of Council Regulation (EC) No 1234/2007 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-86c4f034e565526734f06ae1283fd023] *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed][^f00026]. *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

### Article 44 (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note). Entry into force and application (Extent: W) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_F_4beba8c3-3586-478b-98d7-d66ce87f7681][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed][^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: W)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5dc0968e414e831fbbe6f2e8046051ed]

This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-ded261ec00ccac3746d501384128c8b2]
